        (a_10[shift_sint32(C, x_5)] + a_10[shift_sint32(W, x_6)])) /\
       (a_10[shift_sint32(W, i_1)] =
        (a_10[shift_sint32(W, x_5)] + (2 * a_10[shift_sint32(C, x_6)])))))).
  (* Invariant *)

      (a_10[shift_sint32(C, i_1 - 2)] <= a_10[shift_sint32(C, i_1)]))).
  (* Invariant *)
  Have: forall i_1 : Z. ((i_1 <= w) -> ((2 <= i_1) ->
      (a_10[shift_sint32(C, i_1)] =
       (a_10[shift_sint32(C, i_1 - 2)] + a_10[shift_sint32(W, i_1 - 1)])))).

      (0 <= a_10[shift_sint32(W, i_1)]))).
